Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a hyperbolic algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 29. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 553/4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_29(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 29. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^553(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the hyperbolic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 70. The moduli space has dimension at most 330 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the hyperbolic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^70 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 330-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Statement: For any hyperbolic module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 787. The projective resolution has length at most 549 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
